minimal data set:
=================

fetch('https://now-api.fullscreen.nz/v5/live-epg')
  .then(res => res.json())
  .then(data => data.channels)
  .then(channels => channels.map(channel => {
    return {
      id:         channel.channelId,
      name:       channel.displayName,
      video_url:  channel.videoRenditions.hlsUrl,
      video_type: 'application/x-mpegurl'
    }
  }))
  .then(channels => console.log(JSON.stringify(channels, null, 2)))

stream test: fv23-*
===================

curl -I --insecure "https://fv23-eden.fullscreen.nz/index.m3u8"
  => 403 (eden)

curl -I --insecure "https://fv23-rush.fullscreen.nz/index.m3u8"
  => 403 (Rush)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

observations:
=============

* logged into website using username/password
* the same streams that return 403 with curl cannot play in the web browser
  - network tab shows that the HLS video URLs being requested
    are the same as the ones that I'm testing..
  - this must be a VPN issue..
    * all tests to this point have used: Windscribe (Auckland-Parnell)

outcome:
========

* yep.. it was my VPN
  - Cloudflare must be able to detect Windscribe
  - I switched to: Getflix (Canada w/ Smart DNS)..
    worked like a charm

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

* the URL for browser request #1 is:

      .videoRenditions.lsai.csab
* the URL in the response for browser request #1 is the same as:
    data.channels
      .find(obj => obj.channelId === 'three')
      .videoRenditions.lsai.ssab
  however:
    it appends a querystring parameter: ?aws.sessionId=xxxxx
* the URL for browser request #2 requires this querystring parameter
